## Approval rules
- Core architecture requires clear classical East Asian identity for automatic approval.
- Generic wooden/village buildings are retained as visual-review candidates rather than discarded.
- Nature and environmental dressing are accepted by visual/object suitability, independent of source-pack theme.
- Explicit modern, western-medieval, Viking, vehicle, sci-fi and unrelated wildlife assets remain excluded.
